# CarbonTally — Phase 1 → Phase 8-X Feature Completeness & Implementation Matrix

**Audit ID:** `CT-FEATURE-AUDIT-P1-P8X-001`  ·  **Mode:** READ-ONLY FORENSIC AUDIT  ·  **Date:** 2026-09-21  ·  **Auditor:** OHD / OpenHands (independent)  ·  **Type:** forensic inventory, not an implementation task

---

## 1. Executive summary

This audit answers one question: **what does CarbonTally have today, versus what the documented product requirements from Phase 1 through Phase 8-X say it should have?** It inventories the documentation tree, extracts the documented requirements with citations, traces them against the current code, database, frontend and tests, and records — per requirement — the implementation status, the verification status, the supporting evidence, the gap, and the action needed.

**Scale of the result:** **447 material requirement records** across Phases 1–8-X (plus cross-phase PO decisions, the 16 ADR-V3 decisions and the D-series actor/access decisions), each with an authority tier, a current-requirement classification, an implementation status, a verification status and cited evidence. Requirement rows carry their own citation to a document and section/line; implementation and verification columns carry repository, database, routing or report evidence gathered by this audit.

**Principal findings (evidence-backed):**

1. **The platform is substantially built** — 575-endpoint HTTP surface in the release runtime, 136 public tables, 74 `/api…` router prefixes across two API layers, 22 Phase-8 migrations, ~646 markdown documents. Large parts of Phases 1–8 have code evidence.
2. **The dominant verification gap is not implementation, it is independence.** 260 rows (of 447) rest on Cline implementation/gate reports only. Independent (OHD) verification exists for a narrow, identifiable set: the DEMO-T3 calculation journey (R-A), the factor-provider wiring (G-1), the F-039-1 adjudication lifecycle, the RLS hold register, the consultant/white-label blind-spot audit, and QA-AI-001 (Insight natural-language query).
3. **Documentation and code disagree in both directions.** The audit records **37 requirement rows that are themselves `PO DECISION REQUIRED`**, plus a separate contradiction register in §18 — 57 entries in total (extracted document-vs-document conflicts plus cross-corpus naming/phase conflicts and document-vs-code findings). It includes which phase is "Phase 5/6/7/8", whether X2 alerting was blocked or PO-closed, and whether the B/P implementation was authorised by a Gate-5 instrument or a blanket authorisation.
4. **Legacy surfaces were deliberately retained.** Phase 1's stated non-goal ("do not delete legacy code") means `backend/routes/**` is still mounted alongside `backend/api/**`, the `beta` router is still registered, and PO §9.1's legacy-route retirement is `NOT IMPLEMENTED`. ADR-V3-016 is `DEFERRED`.
5. **19 tables have no code reference at all** and 3 more are referenced only by tests (§17 group I, item 1). Among them are tables the documented configuration architecture says to reuse (`sla_definitions`, `business_hours`) — so ADR-V3-006's "reuse existing" position is not wired.
6. **CarbonTally Insight (Phase 8 D2) is ratified but only partly built.** D2 is documentation-only. I1 (Layer-1 persistence) was committed by another agent during this audit (`5633798`) and its migration is **not applied to the Demo Lab database** (`carbontally_insight_conversations` absent). I2–I8 are `NOT IMPLEMENTED`; D2 §23.5 requires separate authorisation per stage. Per this audit's brief, independent verification of I1 was **explicitly excluded** and is therefore recorded as `Cline implementation report only`.
7. **Capability statements already in the repository are partly stale.** The 2026-08-26 FAQ capability matrix records "customer final approval (no UI)", "custom emission factors (no UI)" and "report PDF download (no button)"; current code contains a customer review UI, a customer-factors tab and a report download control in `ReportDetailPage.jsx`. Conversely the matrix's "automated OCR" *future* item is now implemented (RapidOCR-onnx + Tesseract fallback).

**Method guardrails:** no application code, migration, schema, RLS, configuration, test or data was modified; no defect was fixed; no Insight stage was verified or progressed. The only repository change is this report.

---

## 2. Audit scope

**In scope:** the documented product requirements of Phases 1 → 8-X, their authority tier, their current implementation state in the repository/database/runtime, their verification state, the gaps, and the documentation/code contradictions. Plus reverse analysis (§12 of the brief): documented features with no implementation, backend capability with no frontend exposure, orphan API prefixes, dormant database structures, mock/demo surfaces, duplicate implementations.

**Out of scope / not performed:** any fix, implementation, migration, seed, schema change, configuration change, PO closure, or verification of another agent's in-progress Insight I1 work (explicitly excluded). No ranking of feature importance, no roadmap, no timeline, no investor-readiness judgement.

**Documentation source:** `/home/shomonrobie/carbon_tally/docs` (849 files, 583 markdown, 51 MB) as directed, read-only — that working tree contains another agent's uncommitted changes and was **not** modified. **Implementation source:** the clean, committed release line `/home/shomonrobie/ct_93d5cdd` (branch `p8-release-reconciled`), which is 159 commits ahead of `main`; committed `main` (`20b7a92`) and the Commit/push divergence are recorded in §3 and §21.

## 5. Requirement-authority methodology

1. **Tier first.** Each row is assigned the authority tier of its source using the repository's own hierarchy (`docs/CARBONTALLY_V3_REFERENCE_INDEX.md` §1, L16–28): 1 PO decision · 2 architecture/current implementation · 3 implementation/verification evidence · 4 security/regulatory audit evidence · 5 research/recommendation · 6 historical/superseded.
2. **Evidence ≠ requirement.** Tier-3/4 documents (Cline reports, audits) are treated as *claims to verify*, never as requirements and never as independent verification.
3. **Current-requirement classification** is recorded separately from authority: `current`, `deferred`, `blocked`, `superseded`, `documentation-only`, `not authorised`, or `PO DECISION REQUIRED`.
4. **Implementation status is assigned only from artefact evidence**: a route registration, service/domain/data module, migration/table, frontend component or test located in the audited baseline; or a documented decision that has no implementation surface (explicitly labelled `N/A — POLICY/PRODUCT DECISION`).
5. **Verification status is never inherited from implementation.** Where the only evidence is Cline's own report, the row says so; where no verification exists it says so.
6. **Conflicts are recorded, not resolved** — every documented disagreement is flagged `CONFLICT — PO DECISION REQUIRED` with both citations (§18), and unresolved terminology/scope conflicts are also listed in §22.
7. **Row-level convention.** Requirement text and citation are row-specific (from the documentation extract). Implementation/verification evidence is assigned at **feature-area** level (the migration, module, route family or table that realises the area) and is cited on every row of that area; this is stated here so no row is read as a line-by-line functional test of that requirement.

---
## 6. Phase 1 findings — backend consolidation and core workflow restoration

**Documented objective** (Tier 3 phase reports + Tier 2 closure record): establish one V3 FastAPI composition root, mount the existing V3 router set, restore the core workflow, and **explicitly not** delete legacy code (`CARBONTALLY_PHASE1-6_CLOSURE_AND_PHASE7_CONTINUITY_20260911.md`; `CarbonTally_V3_Phase1_Backend_Consolidation_Report_v1.0.md`).

**Traced implementation.** `backend/main.py` contains exactly one `FastAPI(` composition root and mounts `api_router` at line 265, alongside 40+ legacy routers at lines 212–259 — the consolidation landed and the retention non-goal was honoured. Code-derived route inventory: 2,000+ route decorators across `backend/api/**` and `backend/routes/**` (two API layers).

**Gaps / notes.** The retained legacy layer is the origin of two later findings: PO §9.1 legacy-route retirement is `NOT IMPLEMENTED` (routers still mounted) and 51 of 74 `/api…` prefixes have no literal consumer string in `frontend/src` (partly explained by the separate `admin/` app — see §17).

*Rows: 57 · implementation: IMPLEMENTED 37 · PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED 17 · INSUFFICIENT EVIDENCE 2 · NOT IMPLEMENTED 1 · current-requirement: current 49 · blocked 5 · deferred 3*

## 7. Phase 2 findings — product requirement completion and configuration architecture

**Documented objective:** complete the D–M product requirement set over the existing queue/SLA/capacity configuration (`queue_settings`, `sla_definitions`, `staff_workload`, `business_hours`) without duplicating systems.

**Traced implementation.** `queue_settings` is referenced by 9 backend modules and `staff_workload` by 6 — the queue/capacity half is wired. **`sla_definitions` and `business_hours` have zero code references** and 0 rows: the SLA/business-hours half of the documented configuration architecture is not wired anywhere, which also means ADR-V3-006's "reuse existing" position is aspirational in that half. `work_item_assignments` is wired (`backend/data/manual_extraction.py`).

**Discrepancy recorded by the documentation extract:** no authoritative Phase-1 product artefact exists under the roadmap numbering, so "Phase 1" means backend consolidation in one document family and something else in another (see §18 C-P2-1).

*Rows: 23 · implementation: IMPLEMENTED 12 · PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED 11 · current-requirement: current 9 · deferred 5 · PO DECISION REQUIRED 3 · documentation-only-non-normative 2 · blocked 2 · superseded 2*

## 8. Phase 3 findings — runtime stability and fail-closed routing

**Documented objective:** remove the frontend probe chain, introduce explicit auth error classification (auth-service outage vs credential failure), and make `/login` + `/auth/callback` the single entry points.

**Traced implementation.** The auth guard stack exists (`backend/api/dependencies.py`, `require_*` and `ensure_org_access` helpers); the customer portal runs against the lab gateway with explicit-origin CORS (`backend/config.py` `ALLOWED_ORIGINS`, mirrored by DR-003's gateway change); authenticated journeys were browser-verified by OHD in DR-002/DR-004.

**Residual risk carried forward.** Legacy `roles`-table and name-string `admin`/`is_admin` authorization checks remain in the codebase, which is exactly the risk D18/D20 (actor/access model) record as an open business decision required **before** further entity provisioning.

*Rows: 18 · implementation: IMPLEMENTED 9 · PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED 9 · current-requirement: current 16 · blocked 1 · deferred 1*

## 9. Phase 4 findings — individual user architecture (design gate)

**Documented objective:** decide whether an "individual" user becomes a personal workspace. The design document is explicitly a gate: *"DESIGN-BEFORE-IMPLEMENTATION GATE — no implementation performed"*, status `PHASE 4 DESIGN COMPLETE — AWAITING PRODUCT OWNER APPROVAL`, tier 5 (recommendation).

**Traced implementation.** No personal-workspace code path exists (no personal data/report/subscription ownership surface). The recommendation (do **not** implement a personal workspace) is unimplemented **by design**, so the correct status is `NOT IMPLEMENTED (design gate)` with the decision outstanding — not a defect.

*Rows: 22 · implementation: NOT IMPLEMENTED 11 · PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED 8 · IMPLEMENTED 3 · current-requirement: PO DECISION REQUIRED 8 · current 8 · blocked 2 · documentation-only-non-normative 2 · deferred 2*

## 10. Phase 5 findings — D38/D39/D40 (assignment attribution, messaging boundaries, notifications)

**Documented objective:** make the per-item Work Item real — claim/assign/reassign with an append-only assignment ledger, bounded messaging, and notification fan-out — with the WS4 acceptance gates as the recorded acceptance layer.

**Traced implementation.** `supabase/migrations/20260902030000_phase5_work_item_assignments.sql` creates the append-only `work_item_assignments` ledger with a partial unique index on the open assignment, and `backend/data/manual_extraction.py` reads/writes it (1,100+ lines in that module); `v3_messaging`, `v3_notifications`, `v3_processing*` routers exist. So the core D38 capability is implemented.

**Contradictions (see §18).** The Phase-5 document set contradicts itself on whether a batch may carry several simultaneously effective processing parties: WS4 final acceptance records "BLOCKED — ARCHITECTURAL CONFLICT", while the WS4 Gate-3 acceptance records 63 checks / 0 failures / "GATE 3 — PASSED"; and on whether the entity-origin `calculation_snapshots.source_item_id` residual was fixed (final acceptance records it as a residual defect; Gate-4 re-verifies it as PASS). Both are `PO DECISION REQUIRED`. Additionally the phase-numbering itself is contested (see §18 C-P5-1).

*Rows: 23 · implementation: PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED 19 · IMPLEMENTED 4 · current-requirement: current 18 · superseded 1 · PO DECISION REQUIRED 1 · deferred 1 · blocked 1 · superseded (numbering); content non-normative for Phase 5 D38/D39/D40 1*

## 11. Phase 6 findings — consultant as a first-class operating actor

**Documented objective:** make the consultant a first-class actor with a commercial model, portfolio and client management, plus P0 commercial clarifications; the white-label foundation is D21's commercial consequence.

**Traced implementation.** `backend/api/v3_consultants.py` declares 32 routes; `v3_commercial.py`, `v3_whitelabel.py`, `v3_billing.py` exist; `consultant_profiles.white_label_enabled` (the smallest schema change D21 records) is present; `frontend/src/v3/consultant/**` provides the consultant workspace. The commercial/billing chain is substantial but the *white-label completeness* is explicitly partial: D21.6/D21.8/D21.9 are `PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED` and D21.11 (custom domains) / D21.12 (logo storage) are `FUTURE`; D19's consultant→client transition and export/import are `FUTURE / NOT IMPLEMENTED`.

**Independent evidence.** The OHD consultant/white-label blind-spot audit (`CT-SYSTEM-BLINDSPOTS-CONSULTANT-WHITELABEL-AUDIT-20260914-001.md`) and the consultant-client parity reconciliation (`CT-P8-CURRENT-STATE-GAP-RECONCILIATION-AND-CONSULTANT-CLIENT-PARITY-20260915.md`) are independent findings; several of their items remain open.

## 12. Phase 7 findings — auditor / assurance auditability

**Documented objective:** deliver auditability — a canonical audit taxonomy (category, origin, outcome, actor type), audit trail surfaces, and an evidence package.

**Traced implementation.** `GET /api/v3/exports/audit-package.json` exists (`backend/api/v3_exports.py:90` → `repos.reporting.audit_package`); `backend/api/v3_reporting.py` (last touched by commit `4368157` "auditor/assurance auditability, taxonomy") exists; `audit_trail` is present with a non-internal immutability trigger, and R-1's independent R-A verification exercised the audit chain end-to-end on a live correction event.

**Contradiction.** The closure document records Phase 7 = Auditor/Assurance, **CLOSED**; the Cline `CARBONTALLY_V3_PHASE_7_REPORT.md` records Phase 7 = Consultant / multi-client with the runtime BLOCKED and Phase 8 = internal operations/QC (see §18 C-P5-3).

*Rows: 8 · implementation: IMPLEMENTED 6 · PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED 2 · current-requirement: current 5 · current (with accepted residual) 1 · deferred 1 · superseded (numbering); content non-normative for Phase 7 1*

## 13. Phase 8 findings — reporting, disclosure, RLS, extraction remediation, factors, adjudication, Insight

Phase 8 is the largest and most contested band: **164 requirement rows** in this audit. It is summarised here in its sub-streams; the rows follow in §15 and the conflicts in §18.

### 13.1 B1–B4 (disclosure model, evidence line items, provenance links, intensity, narrative overlay, frozen artefact)

All four B stages have implementation artefacts: migrations `20260914000000_p8_b1_disclosure_model_foundation`, `20260915000000_p8_b1_correction_privileges_and_evidence_idempotency`, `20260916000000_p8_b2_evidence_line_items`, `20260916010000_p8_b2_provenance_line_links`, `20260917000000_p8_b3_intensity_catalogue`, `20260917010000_p8_b3_intensity_ratios`, `20260918000000_p8_b4_narrative_overlay`, `20260919000000_p8_b4_frozen_artefact`, plus `backend/api/v3_disclosure.py` and commit `ae5d3bd` ("implement B2 evidence-line provenance"). Independent verification: none specific to B1–B4. The B1 disclosure stream carries the **A-RLS** open decision (report-table RLS policy coverage) which B1's own contract records as gating S2.

### 13.2 P1 — PDF/image extraction remediation

Implemented in `backend/services/automatic_extraction.py`: OCR for image/scan documents plus a pure-pip ONNX fallback (`RapidOCR`) with pypdfium2 rendering when Tesseract is unavailable, and bounded execution in `services/automatic_processing.py`. Independent verification exists at the pipeline level: R-A recovered the supplier from the rendered page 1 of `t3imp_uk-gas__ORG_022_british_gas_202511.pdf` through the OCR path and traced the result to snapshot `af640887`. Earlier production verification of the P1 row-candidate detection was recorded as `BLOCKED: no authorized session`, which is a *production* environment limit, not a lab one.

### 13.3 P2 — EF-E qualified unit selection

`backend/engines/factor_selection_policy.py` exists and the policy is exercised by the factor-context tests verified in G-1. **Cross-branch divergence:** `main` contains `a5e8cc1 feat(p8): publish P2 EF-E qualified unit selection` and `5f3d477 test(p8): align MemoryFactors fake with EF-E selector contract` which are **not** in the audited release branch — so the release line does not contain the latest EF-E publication. This is an evidence limitation, not a defect claim.

### 13.4 S-series — report lifecycle

`20260913000000_p8_report_lifecycle_status.sql` and `20260921000000_p8_s2_is_current_single_valued.sql` implement the vocabulary observed in code: `DRAFT`, `REVIEWED`, `CHANGES_REQUESTED`, `REJECTED`, `APPROVED` (counts 11/8/4/9/9 occurrences in `backend/api/v3_reports.py` + services), `report_version_artifacts` for frozen artefacts, `backend/api/v3_reports.py` (S6 lifecycle visibility, commit `eb6a0c2`) and a `report-lifecycle-panel` frontend test. The S2 boundary is recorded as gated by the open A-RLS decision; S3–S8 status is not established by this audit.

### 13.5 RLS remediation (4a1b / 4a2 / 4b)

Four containment/hardening migrations exist (`20260920000000_p8_rls_anon_grant_containment`, `20260922000000_p8_rls_4a2_authenticated_grant_hardening`, `20260923000000_p8_rls_4a1b_anon_default_privilege_hardening`, `20260925000000_p8_rls_4b_group1_enablement`). The `group1` suffix is material: only RLS **group 1** is enabled, and the B1 disclosure stream records that report tables without an `organization_id` carry no policy at all (deny-all to `authenticated`, with backend access only because the connecting role bypasses RLS). The OHD RLS hold register remains the independent artefact for this area.

### 13.6 FIN-06 manual-processing governance and FS activity-clarification/adjudication

`20260927000000_p8_fin06_manual_processing_governance.sql` with the admin-gated `backend/api/manual_processing_admin.py`; and the FS stream (`20260928000000_p8_fs_activity_clarifications` … `20260931000000_p8_fs_adjudication_context_lineage`, `backend/engines/activity_clarification.py`, `backend/api/v3_activity_clarifications.py`) which carries **the strongest independent verification in Phase 8**: the F-039-1 adjudication-lifecycle report (`docs/ohd/reports/CT-F039-1-ADJUDICATION-LIFECYCLE-INDEPENDENT-VERIFICATION-20260919-064.md`).

### 13.7 D4 emission-factor internal containment

`20260926000000_p8_d4_emission_factors_internal_containment.sql` — present; no independent verification.

### 13.8 CarbonTally Insight (D2 + I1…I8)

**Ratified architecture (Tier 1, documentation-only).** D2 ratifies naming (`CarbonTally Insight`, canonical `carbontally_insight_*`; D1's `ask_*` superseded), persistence (YES), the separate-domain rule (vs Human↔Human messaging), the three-layer persistence model, the authorization boundary, the creator-private visibility model for the first customer-workspace surface, deterministic-first, RAG deferred, LangChain deferred, the LLM never being an authorization boundary, and the staged plan `I1…I8` — with §23.5: *each stage requires its own authorisation*.

**Implementation state.**

| Stage | Authorised? | Implemented? | Evidence |
|---|---|---|---|
| I1 — persistence foundation | Ambiguous (`CT-P8-SPEC-001` C-1: `d91ace5` subject "authorize I1" vs D2 "ready for I1 … authorisation"; §24.4 requires a separate prompt) | **Code present** — `5633798`: `backend/api/v3_insight.py` (5 routes: POST/GET conversations, GET conversation, POST/GET messages), `backend/domain/insight.py`, `backend/data/insight.py`, migration `20261001000000_p8_i1_insight_persistence.sql` (2 tables + 4 creator-scoped RLS policies), 2 test modules | **Migration not applied to the Demo Lab DB** (`carbontally_insight_conversations` absent, checked 2026-09-21) |
| I2 — authorization/visibility | No | No | — |
| I3 — controlled read-only tools + deterministic path | No | No | — |
| I4 — AI interaction records + canonical audit (+ dormant-AI-table decision §22) | No | No | `ai_content_history` still dormant (0 rows, no application reference) |
| I5 — context management | No | No | — |
| I6 — Insight UI | No | No | No authenticated Insight surface in `frontend/src/v3/**` |
| I7 — privacy/retention/export | No | No | — |
| I8 — billing/production hardening (incl. §14.4 usage/cost gap) | No | No | `LLMClient.complete()` returns text only — gap independently confirmed |

**Independent verification of I1: excluded by this audit's brief.** The row therefore reads `Cline implementation report only`. For completeness of the historical record: QA-AI-001 (`docs/verification/QA-AI-001-…md`) verified at HEAD `770046a` that no `carbontally_insight_*` object existed then, and that the only natural-language surface was the frontend-only public FAQ assistant (0 backend calls).

## 14. Phase 8-X findings — operational intelligence (X1–X8)

**Documented objective:** operational intelligence for the running platform — worker/queue visibility, health correctness, aggregation, ops console, thresholds/alerting, API runtime metrics, verification.

**Traced implementation (present):** `backend/api/v3_operations.py` (operations surface), `backend/domain/operational_health.py`, `backend/services/operational_intelligence.py` (X4, commit `a71a46a`), `backend/services/operational_alerting.py` + `backend/domain/operational_alerts.py` (X2 conditions `QUEUE_BACKLOG`/`WORKER_STALE`/`SLA_BREACH`/`RETRY_EXHAUSTED` with PO constants), `backend/services/retention.py`, `backend/data/api_metrics.py` (X7), migration `20260924000000_p8x_x2_operational_telemetry_retention.sql`, and the X5 frontend ops console (`frontend/src/v3/ops/OperationsPage.jsx`, `OperationalHealthTab.jsx`, commit `137765f`) with its own vitest suite. Commit `20d27c0` banked X1 health + X2 alerting.

**Contradictions (see §18).** The X-series identifiers do not mean the same thing across documents (discovery §15 vs the X1 feasibility doc vs the X2 contract vs the P8X readiness batch split); the X2 contract states implementation is **BLOCKED at the `PX-6` gate** while the X5 contract (later) states "X1, X2, X4 — all IMPLEMENTED, INDEPENDENTLY VERIFIED, PO-CLOSED"; and the retention-scope exclusion (X2-D7, work records outside any retention rule) is treated as settled by X4 while X2 recorded it open. X8 is recorded elsewhere as "undefined as an artefact".

## 15. Master feature matrix

Legend and honesty notes:

* **Authority Status** is the tier of the source document, using the repository's own hierarchy (`CARBONTALLY_V3_REFERENCE_INDEX.md` §1): `T1` PO decision · `T2` architecture/current implementation · `T3` implementation/verification evidence · `T4` security/regulatory audit evidence · `T5` research · `T6` historical/superseded. Values such as `T2/DECIDED` retain the source's own status word.
* **Current?** is the current-requirement classification. `PO DECISION REQUIRED` means the documentation cannot settle the requirement (see §18/§22) — it is not a defect claim.
* **Implementation Status** is assigned only from artefact evidence located in the audited baseline, and is assigned at **feature-area** level (see §5.7). `IMPLEMENTED — NOT INDEPENDENTLY VERIFIED` is used wherever code evidence exists but the only verification is an implementation report; `INSUFFICIENT EVIDENCE` is used rather than guessing.
* **Verification Status** is never inherited from implementation. "Cline implementation report only" means exactly that.
* **Requirement Source** cites the document and section/line for the requirement text as extracted; the requirement text itself is a condensation of the source wording.
* Two values outside the brief's taxonomy are used for decisions with no code surface and are labelled explicitly: `N/A — POLICY/PRODUCT DECISION` and `N/A — GOVERNANCE/PROCESS DECISION`.

### I. Reverse-analysis findings (documented requirement ↔ code) — not row-level, from `reverse-analysis.md` and the inventory)

1. **19 tables have no code reference anywhere** (not in application code, not in tests, not in the frontend): `business_hours`, `conversation_activity_log`, `customer_communication`, `file_attachments`, `login_history`, `notification_templates`, `processing_steps`, `processing_time_log`, `product_categories`, `report_comments`, `staff_activity_log`, `staff_daily_performance`, `staff_performance`, `supplier_categories`, `team_performance`, `typing_status`, `user_activity_log`, `user_presence`, `verification_logs`; 3 more are referenced only from tests. Two of them (`sla_definitions`, `business_hours`) are named by ADR-V3-006/P2 as the configuration to reuse — the documented architecture is therefore not wired there.
2. **Two API layers coexist**: `backend/api/**` (v3) and `backend/routes/**` (legacy), both mounted in `backend/main.py`. 51 of 74 `/api…` prefixes have no literal consumer string in `frontend/src` (partly explained by the separate `admin/` app — `carbontally-admin`, homepage `/admin`).
3. **Parallel frontend/duplicate surfaces**: `admin/` (a second React app), `frontend_backup_pre_v3_public_20260827/` (a full frontend copy), `carbon-tally-ui-demo/`, `docs/architecture/UI/` + `UI2/` (static mockups, not the live app), plus `prisma/schema.prisma` (a second schema representation with no code users found).
4. **Mock/demo/placeholder surfaces** (must not be read as production capability): the public website FAQ assistant (`frontend/src/public/assistant/**`) is a frontend-only deterministic FAQ matcher — QA-AI-001 measured **0 network requests** per question and answers cited to `Source: CarbonTally Customer FAQ`; the public homepage's "interactive demonstration" (invoice INV-2026-0417) is static marketing content; the Demo Lab (`tools/demo_lab/**`, `demodatagen/**`) is a synthetic environment with explicitly seeded identities and factor data (DEMO-T1/T2-C); `ai_content_history` is a dormant AI table.
5. **Capability-matrix drift** (documented 2026-08-26, checked against current code): "customer final approval (no UI)" — a customer review UI now exists (`frontend/src/v3/customer/ReviewPage.jsx`, `ReviewDetailPage.jsx`); "custom emission factors (full API; no UI)" — a `customer-factors-tab` frontend test now exists; "report PDF download (endpoint exists; no button)" — `ReportDetailPage.jsx` now has a download control (`downloadReport`); and the matrix's *future* item "automated OCR" is now implemented (RapidOCR-onnx + Tesseract). Conversely the matrix's "no customer UI for item-level processing" claim still matches the code state it describes (`ProcessingItemWorkspace.jsx` exists but the matrix predates it) — the matrix is partially stale in both directions.
6. **Feature-flag / configuration surface**: the backend reads a small environment surface (`CARBONTALLY_AI_*`, database/storage and app-config variables); no flag was found that disables a documented capability wholesale. The AI provider gate is the one capability switch, and it is **off in the lab** (three variables unset), so all AI-attributed behaviour there is the deterministic path.

## 18. Documentation / code contradictions

Every entry below is a **document-vs-document or document-vs-code disagreement found during this audit**. None is resolved here: the audit records both positions with citations and flags `CONFLICT — PO DECISION REQUIRED`, exactly as the brief requires. Where the contradiction is between a document and the code, the current repository state is stated and the historical claim is preserved.

### 18.1 Scope, naming and phase-numbering conflicts (cross-corpus)

- **Which phase is "Phase 5"?** — Phase 5 = D38/D39/D40 (attribution, messaging boundaries, notifications) per `architecture/CARBONTALLY_PHASE5_D38_D39_D40_PREIMPLEMENTATION_PLAN.md` tl./§1 vs Phase 5 = Reporting (reports dashboard, `/api/v3/reports`, versions, download, exports) per `audit/cline/CARBONTALLY_V3_PHASE_5_REPORT.md` tl.13/§1. `CONFLICT — PO DECISION REQUIRED`
- **Which phase is "Phase 6"?** — Consultant Workflow (`CARBONTALLY_PHASE6_CLOSURE_AND_RELEASE_BOUNDARY_20260911.md` §1–§2) vs Customer Administration (`audit/cline/CARBONTALLY_V3_PHASE_6_REPORT.md` tl.14, whose §25 makes Phase 7 = Consultants). `CONFLICT — PO DECISION REQUIRED`
- **Which phase is "Phase 7"?** — Auditor/Assurance, CLOSED (`CARBONTALLY_PHASE7_CLOSURE_AND_RELEASE_BOUNDARY_20260912.md` §1) vs Consultant/multi-client, runtime BLOCKED (`audit/cline/CARBONTALLY_V3_PHASE_7_REPORT.md` tl.12, §17, §23). `CONFLICT — PO DECISION REQUIRED`
- **What does "Phase 8" mean?** — Internal Operations/Processing/QC, "IMPLEMENTED" (`audit/cline/CARBONTALLY_V3_PHASE_8_REPORT.md` tl.1-10, §0) vs the Reporting/Disclosure programme with "Phase 8 — Advanced Analytics" preserved as the historical master-roadmap label (`CARBONTALLY_PHASE8_DISCLOSURE_MODEL_DECISION_RECORD_20260912.md` §1.1; G0 register §1 G0-B). G0-B is CLOSED for implementation tasks; the historical document was never retitled, so whole-corpus search remains ambiguous (residual PX-1). `CONFLICT — PO DECISION REQUIRED`
- **Phase 8-X vs Phase 9** — `CARBONTALLY_PHASE8X_…DISCOVERY…md` §2/§17 (8-X is not a new Phase 9; historical Phase-9 labels are lineage only) vs the existence of `CARBONTALLY_PHASE9_SYSTEM_RUNTIME_AND_OPERATIONAL_INTELLIGENCE_BASELINE_20260912.md` and migration `20260822000000_p9_rls_recursion_fix.sql`, plus a later PO ruling (`CT-P8-P8X-PHASE9-PO-RECONCILIATION-20260913-040.md`) that "there is NO Phase 9 for this programme". `CONFLICT — PO DECISION REQUIRED`
- **X-series stage numbering** — discovery §15 (X1 foundation … X8 verification) vs X1 feasibility (X1 = worker/queue visibility + health) vs X2 contract (X2 = threshold evaluation + alert dispatch = discovery's X6) vs P8X readiness §20 (batch X1 = stages X1–X3, batch X2 = stages X4–X7). The same identifiers denote different scopes. `CONFLICT — PO DECISION REQUIRED`
- **Insight: persistence vs session-bounded design (R-1)** — `audit/openhands/CARBONTALLY_V3_AI_ASSISTANT_ARCHITECTURE.md` §9/§10.2 (bounded context per session, short-lived sessions) vs D2 §5.1 (persistent storage) and §25.1–§25.3 (records the supersession but forbids rewriting the older document). Declared non-blocking for I1 at D2 L1401. `CONFLICT — PO DECISION REQUIRED`
- **Insight: "PE" terminology (TERM-1)** — D2 §10.5 records the prompt heading "Private Equity" while the ratified model uses PE = Processing Entity; states an investor-side persona does not exist and would need a separate PO decision; `PO CLARIFICATION REQUIRED (non-blocking for I1)`. `CONFLICT — PO DECISION REQUIRED`
- **Insight: I1 authorisation instrument (C-1)** — Commit `d91ace5` subject "docs: ratify CarbonTally Insight architecture and authorize I1" vs D2 §24.4 "A separate prompt must authorise I1 implementation" and status "READY FOR I1 IMPLEMENTATION AUTHORISATION"; `CT-P8-SPEC-001` §M C-1 records the conflict; the I1 code was nevertheless committed (this audit records the fact, not a position). `CONFLICT — PO DECISION REQUIRED`

### 18.2 Documentation vs code (current state vs historical claim)

- **Legacy route retirement** — PO register §9.1 "LOCKED" retirement direction vs `backend/main.py:212-259` still mounting 40+ legacy routers from `backend/routes/**` (including `legacy_reports.router`) and ADR-V3-016 `DEFERRED`. Current state: **not retired**.
- **Beta infrastructure** — PO register §9.2 (obsolete beta infrastructure to be removed) vs `beta` router still registered (`backend/main.py:239`) and `/api/admin/beta` present. Current state: **still present**.
- **ADR-V3-009 issue management** — ADR register §4 L110 records "Implementation pending" vs the `issues` table and `issues_router` registration (`backend/api/router.py:200`) plus entity-scoped issue mediation. Current state: **implemented; the register text is behind the code**.
- **FAQ capability matrix (2026-08-26) vs current code** — "Customer final approval — no UI", "custom emission factors — no UI", "report PDF download — no button" vs `frontend/src/v3/customer/ReviewPage.jsx` + `ReviewDetailPage.jsx`, a `customer-factors-tab` test, and `downloadReport` in `frontend/src/v3/reports/ReportDetailPage.jsx`. Also "automated OCR" listed as future vs the implemented RapidOCR/Tesseract path. Current state: **the matrix is partially stale** (the matrix is itself labelled AUDIT EVIDENCE, so this is drift, not a defect claim).
- **Report-table RLS (Part A C1)** — `CARBONTALLY_PHASE8_PRODUCT_AND_REPORT_RATIFICATION_20260912.md` §8.4/§22.2 D-12/§25.3 defect 1 (report tables have no RLS/policy) vs `CARBONTALLY_PHASE8_OPEN_DECISION_CLOSURE_AND_IMPLEMENTATION_AUTHORISATION_20260912.md` §4.1–§4.3/§4.10 (the schema-wide enablement loop means RLS **is** enabled on all four report tables, but `report_versions`/`report_comments` have no `organization_id` and therefore no policy; backend access works only because the connecting role bypasses RLS), plus a third document recording the wider baseline ("RLS enabled on ~7/104 production tables; 97 disabled; `anon` holds `GRANT ALL`"). B requires correction notes in the three earlier documents; that revision has not been performed.
- **Phase 2 configuration tables** — ADR-V3-006/P2 say `sla_definitions`/`business_hours` exist and are to be reused vs **zero code references** and 0 rows for both tables in the audited baseline. Current state: **not wired**.
- **Insight implementation status over time** — QA-AI-001 (this auditor, HEAD `770046a`) records that no `carbontally_insight_*` object exists anywhere vs the current HEAD containing `backend/api/v3_insight.py`, `domain/insight.py`, `data/insight.py` and migration `20261001000000_p8_i1_insight_persistence.sql` (Cline `5633798`). Both statements are true of their respective HEADs; the current state additionally shows the **migration unapplied in the lab DB**.
- **ADR-V3-003/004/005/007/011/013 provisional decisions** — Six ADRs remain `PROVISIONALLY DECIDED` in a register the index calls "authoritative for current design", while the corresponding capability areas (Work Item abstraction, dpq producer, duplicate history surfaces, auto-assignment/skills, work types, history consolidation) show partial or untraced implementation. `PO/architecture decision needed` rather than a code fix.

## 21. Evidence limitations

1. **The runtime was down during the audit** (`127.0.0.1:8070` refused connections). The audit therefore derived the route surface from committed code; the earlier live-OpenAPI reading (575 paths) is cited as prior evidence with its timestamp and was not re-taken.
2. **The lab database is not production.** Row counts in the lab (`107 of 136 tables empty`) are used only for dormancy corroboration alongside code-reference analysis; they are **not** statements about production data. The reference index records the production `emission_factors` population as 7,049 rows, which this audit did not read.
3. **Documentation was read from a working tree containing another agent's uncommitted changes** (`/home/shomonrobie/carbon_tally`, 298 modified + 791 untracked). That tree was read-only for this audit; the documentation inventory therefore reflects that tree at the time of reading, not a committed state.
4. **Cross-branch divergence.** `main` carries four commits not on the audited release branch (`20b7a92`, `5f3d477`, `a5e8cc1`, `f671ec3`) — including the P2 EF-E publication — so some capability states differ between the release line and `main`. The audit reports the release line; where a `main`-only artefact is material it is named.
5. **Requirement text is condensed** from the source documents by the audit's documentation extracts; section/line citations are provided so any row can be checked against the source. Implementation/verification columns are assigned at feature-area level (§5.7), so a row states the area's evidence, not a bespoke test of that individual line.
6. **Some areas were traced at file/route/table level only.** Where the audit could not establish an artefact, the row says `INSUFFICIENT EVIDENCE` rather than guessing (4 rows).
7. **Working extracts (not committed)**: `audit_inputs/P1-P4-documented-requirements.md`, `P5-P7-documented-requirements.md`, `P8A-documented-requirements.md`, `P8B-reporting-rls-requirements.md`, `P8-Insight-P8X-requirements.md`, `implementation-inventory.md`, `reverse-analysis.md` under `/home/shomonrobie/workspace/project/0000789dfc0f47f790dcfe312824eb99/`. Only this report is committed.

## 22. PO decisions required

Evidence-backed, no recommendation adopted. Grouped by the decision's blocking effect.

1. **Insight I1 authorisation instrument** — Is I1 implemented under a valid authorisation? `d91ace5`'s subject claims it; D2 §24.4 requires a separate prompt; `CT-P8-SPEC-001` C-1 records the conflict. The code exists and its migration is unapplied in the lab.
2. **Insight I2–I8 authorisation** — D2 §23.5: each stage requires its own authorisation. None is authorised.
3. **Report-table RLS (`A-RLS`)** — Which of the three documented positions on report-table RLS/policy coverage is correct, and when are the three earlier documents corrected? Recorded as gating S2.
4. **RLS group coverage** — Only RLS group 1 is enabled; the remaining groups and the legacy permissive-policy inventory are open (ADR-V3-010 INV-1/INV-2).
5. **X2 alerting status** — Was X2 unblocked after the `PX-6` gate (as X5 asserts, "PO-CLOSED") or is it still blocked (as the X2 contract states)?
6. **X2-D7 retention scope** — Are `document_processing_queue` and `processing_logs` inside or outside the retention rule?
7. **X-series numbering** — Which numbering governs (discovery §15, the contracts, or the P8X batch split)? X8 is "undefined as an artefact".
8. **Phase numbering (5/6/7/8)** — Which phase-name mapping is canonical for the programme record? Both families remain in the corpus and are unresolved.
9. **Phase 8-X vs Phase 9** — Confirm the "no Phase 9" ruling against the surviving Phase-9 baseline document and the `p9_*` migration.
10. **D20 scope-aware staff authorization** — Recorded as required **before** entity provisioning; `roles`-table and name-string `admin`/`is_admin` risks remain in code.
11. **D14/D17 entity pipeline semantics** — Entity assignment on the extraction pipeline and entity-staff processing of assigned work remain implementation gaps against resolved business requirements.
12. **D19 consultant→client transition & export/import** — Recorded `FUTURE / NOT IMPLEMENTED`.
13. **ADR-V3-003/004/005/007/011/013/016** — Six provisionally-decided ADRs plus the deferred queue-retirement chain; the legacy API layer's fate depends on them (PO §9.1).
14. **FAQ capability matrix correction** — The matrix's "no UI" claims for customer final approval, custom factors and report download no longer match the code; correct or annotate.
15. **Phase 4 individual-user recommendation** — Design complete, awaiting approval; no implementation exists either way.
16. **PE terminology (TERM-1)** — D2 §10.5 records the "Private Equity" vs Processing Entity mismatch and states an investor persona would need its own decision.

## 23. Final audit conclusion

The documentation inventory and the implementation trace were performed across Phases 1 → 8-X: 447 requirement records, each with an authority tier, a current-requirement classification, an implementation status, a verification status and cited evidence; plus a reverse analysis (19 unreferenced tables, two API layers, duplicate frontend surfaces, mock/demo surfaces, capability-matrix drift) and a contradiction register spanning both document-vs-document and document-vs-code disagreements.

**What this audit does provide:** an evidence-backed statement of the current documented feature state per phase, an explicit separation of "code exists" / "feature present" / "independently verified", the inventory of what is not implemented, partial, deferred, blocked or unauthorised, and the list of documentation conflicts that only the Product Owner can settle.

**What it does not provide (and does not claim):** functional verification of each capability, a production-data assessment, any priority or roadmap judgement, or verification of the Insight I1 work committed by another agent during the audit (explicitly excluded by the brief).

### Critical unresolved issues (evidence-backed, requiring PO or engineering action)

1. Insight I1 authorisation instrument unresolved while the implementation is committed and its migration is unapplied in the lab database; I2–I8 unauthorised.
2. Report-table RLS coverage (`A-RLS`) unresolved across three documents, recorded as gating S2; only RLS group 1 enabled.
3. X2 alerting status and X2-D7 retention scope unresolved between the X2, X4 and X5 contracts.
4. Phase numbering (5/6/7/8) and the X-series stage numbering unresolved across the corpus.
5. Legacy API layer and beta surfaces still mounted against a LOCKED retirement direction (PO §9.1/§9.2, ADR-V3-016 DEFERRED).
6. D20 scope-aware staff authorization and the `roles`/name-string admin risks outstanding before further entity provisioning.
7. `sla_definitions`/`business_hours` (plus 17 other tables) unreferenced by any code while the documented configuration architecture says to reuse them.
8. The 2026-08-26 FAQ capability matrix is stale in three capabilities and one future item; it is the documented basis for customer-facing claims.

### Final statement

> **This audit does provide sufficient evidence to establish the current documented feature completeness state of CarbonTally from Phase 1 through Phase 8-X** — at the granularity stated in §5.7: every material requirement record carries a cited source, an authority tier, a current-requirement classification, an implementation status grounded in artefact evidence, a separately-stated verification status, and a gap; and the reverse analysis, dormancy analysis and contradiction register bound what is claimed.
>
> **It does not establish** functional correctness of individual capabilities, production data state, capability beyond the released branch, or the state of the Insight I1 work committed by another agent during the audit (verification of which was excluded by the brief). Those remain for targeted verification.
